```solidity
contract BurnableToken is StandardToken {
    event AutoGenerated_mapping_BurnableToken(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);
    using SafeMath for uint256;
    event Burn(address indexed burner, uint256 value);

    function burn(uint256 _value) public {
        if (!(_value > 0)) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(_value <= balances[msg.sender])) {
            revert();
        }
        address burner = msg.sender;
        balances[burner] = balances[burner].sub(_value);
        totalSupply = totalSupply.sub(_value);
        Burn(burner, _value);
        Transfer(burner, address(0x0), _value);
```
```solidity
    }
}

contract BasicToken is ERC20Basic {
    event AutoGenerated_mapping_BasicToken(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);
    using SafeMath for uint256;
    using SafeMath for uint256;
    mapping(address => uint256) internal balances;
    uint256 internal totalSupply_;

    function totalSupply() public view returns (uint256) {
        return totalSupply_;
    }

    function transfer(address _to, uint256 _value) public returns (bool) {
        if (!(_value <= balances[msg.sender])) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(_to != address(0x0))) {
            revert();
        }
        balances[msg.sender] = balances[msg.sender].sub(_value);
        balances[_to] = balances[_to].add(_value);
        emit Transfer(msg.sender, _to, _value);
        return true;
    }

    function balanceOf(address _owner) public view returns (uint256) {
        return balances[_owner];
    }
}

contract StandardToken is ERC20, BasicToken {
    event AutoGenerated_mapping_StandardToken(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);
    using SafeMath for uint256;
    mapping(address => mapping(address => uint256)) internal allowed;

    function transferFrom(address _from, address _to, uint256 _value) public returns (bool) {
        if (!(_value <= balances[_from])) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(_value <= allowed[_from][msg.sender])) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(_to != address(0x0))) {
            revert();
        }
        balances[_from] = balances[_from].sub(_value);
        balances[_to] = balances[_to].add(_value);
        allowed[_from][msg.sender] = allowed[_from][msg.sender].sub(_value);
        emit Transfer(_from, _to, _value);
        return true;
    }

    function approve(address _spender, uint256 _value) public returns (bool) {
        allowed[msg.sender][_spender] = _value;
        emit Approval(msg.sender, _spender, _value);
        return true;
    }

    function allowance(address _owner, address _spender) public view returns (uint256) {
        return allowed[_owner][_spender];
    }

    function increaseApproval(address _spender, uint256 _addedValue) public returns (bool) {
        allowed[msg.sender][_spender] = (allowed[msg.sender][_spender].add(_addedValue));
        emit Approval(msg.sender, _spender, allowed[msg.sender][_spender]);
        return true;
    }

    function decreaseApproval(address _spender, uint256 _subtractedValue) public returns (bool) {
        uint256 oldValue = allowed[msg.sender][_spender];
        if (_subtractedValue >= oldValue) {
            allowed[msg.sender][_spender] = 0;
        } else {
            allowed[msg.sender][_spender] = oldValue.sub(_subtractedValue);
        }
        emit Approval(msg.sender, _spender, allowed[msg.sender][_spender]);
        return true;
    }
}

contract Ownable {
    event AutoGenerated_mapping_Ownable(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);
    address public owner;
    event OwnershipRenounced(address indexed previousOwner);
    event OwnershipTransferred(address indexed previousOwner, address indexed originNewOwner);

    constructor() public {
        owner = msg.sender;
    }

    modifier onlyOwner() {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner)) {
            revert();
        }
        _;
    }

    function renounceOwnership() public onlyOwner {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner)) {
            revert();
        }
        emit OwnershipRenounced(owner);
        owner = address(0x0);
    }

    function transferOwnership(address _newOwner) public onlyOwner {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner)) {
            revert();
        }
        {
            if (!(_newOwner != address(0x0))) {
                revert();
            }
            emit OwnershipTransferred(owner, _newOwner);
            owner = _newOwner;
        }
    }

    function _transferOwnership(address _newOwner) internal {
        if (!(_newOwner != address(0x0))) {
            revert();
        }
        emit OwnershipTransferred(owner, _newOwner);
        owner = _newOwner;
    }
}

    event AutoGenerated_mapping_VaultToken(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);
    using SafeMath for uint256;
    string public name = "Vault Smart Security";

    uint8 public decimals = 18;
    uint public initialSupply = 10000000000 * 10 ** uint256(decimals);
    mapping(address => bool) public frozenAccount;
    event FrozenFunds(address target, bool frozen);

    constructor() public DetailedERC20(name, symbol, decimals) {
        totalSupply_ = initialSupply;
        balances[msg.sender] = totalSupply_;
        emit Transfer(address(0x0), owner, totalSupply_);
    }

    function transfer(address _to, uint256 _value) public returns (bool) {
        {
            if (!(_to != address(0x0))) {
                revert();
            }
            if (!(balances[msg.sender] >= _value)) {
                revert();
            }
            if (!(balances[_to].add(_value) >= balances[_to])) {
                revert();
            }
            if (!(!frozenAccount[msg.sender])) {
                revert();
            }
            if (!(!frozenAccount[_to])) {
                revert();
            }
            balances[msg.sender] = balances[msg.sender].sub(_value);
            balances[_to] = balances[_to].add(_value);
            emit Transfer(msg.sender, _to, _value);
        }
    }

    function _transfer(address _from, address _to, uint _value) internal {
        if (!(_to != address(0x0))) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(balances[_from] >= _value)) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(balances[_to].add(_value) >= balances[_to])) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(!frozenAccount[_from])) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(!frozenAccount[_to])) {
            revert();
        }
        balances[_from] = balances[_from].sub(_value);
        balances[_to] = balances[_to].add(_value);
        emit Transfer(_from, _to, _value);
    }
}
```

```solidity
    event PercentLockRemoved(address target);

            revert();

            revert();

        if (percentLockedAddress[_from] == true) {
            if (!(_value <= percentLockAvailable[_from])) {
                revert();
            }
            percentLockAvailable[_from] = percentLockAvailable[_from].sub(_value);
        }

    function freezeAddress(address target, bool freeze) public onlyOwner {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner)) {
            revert();
        }
        frozenAddress[target] = freeze;
        emit FrozenFunds(target, freeze);
    }

    function PercentLock(address target, uint percentage, uint256 available) public onlyOwner {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner)) {
            revert();
        }
        percentLockedAddress[target] = true;
        percentLockAvailable[target] = available;
        emit PercentLocked(target, percentage, available);
    }

    function removePercentLock(address target) public onlyOwner {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner)) {
            revert();
        }
        percentLockedAddress[target] = false;
        percentLockAvailable[target] = 0;
        emit PercentLockRemoved(target);
    }

    function sendTransfer(address _from, address _to, uint256 _value) external onlyOwner {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner)) {
            revert();
        }
        {
            if (!(_to != address(0x0))) {
                revert();
            }
            if (!(balanceOf[_from] >= _value)) {
                revert();
            }
            if (!(balanceOf[_to].add(_value) >= balanceOf[_to])) {
                revert();

                revert();
            }

                revert();
            }

            balanceOf[_to] = balanceOf[_to].add(_value);
            if (!(balanceOf[_from].add(balanceOf[_to]) == TOSC__transfer_3_previousBalances_0_0)) {
                revert();
            }
            emit Transfer(_from, _to, _value);
        }
    }

    function getBalance(address addr) external view onlyOwner returns (uint256) {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner)) {
            revert();
        }
        return balanceOf[addr];
    }

    function getfrozenAddress(address addr) external view onlyOwner returns (bool) {
```

```solidity
        decimals = 18;
        totalSupply = 20000000000000000000000000000;
        balanceOf[owner] = 20000000000000000000000000000;
    }

    function transfer(address _to, uint256 _value) returns (bool success) {
        if (!(balanceOf[msg.sender] >= _value)) {
            revert();
        }

        balanceOf[_to] += _value;
        Transfer(msg.sender, _to, _value);
        return true;

        return true;
    }

        if (!(balanceOf[_from] >= _value)) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(allowance[_from][msg.sender] >= _value)) {
            revert();
        }
        balanceOf[_from] -= _value;
        balanceOf[_to] += _value;
        allowance[_from][msg.sender] -= _value;
        Transfer(_from, _to, _value);
        return true;
    }

    function burn(uint256 _value) returns (bool success) {
        if (!(balanceOf[msg.sender] >= _value)) {
            revert();
        }
        balanceOf[msg.sender] -= _value;
        totalSupply -= _value;
        Burn(msg.sender, _value);
        return true;
    }

    function burnFrom(address _from, uint256 _value) returns (bool success) {
        if (!(balanceOf[_from] >= _value)) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(msg.sender == owner)) {
            revert();
        }
        balanceOf[_from] -= _value;
        totalSupply -= _value;
        Burn(_from, _value);
        return true;
    }
}
```

```solidity
pragma solidity ^0.4.19;

contract Private_Bank {
    event AutoGenerated_mapping_Private_Bank(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);
    mapping(address => uint) public balances;
    uint public MinDeposit = 1 ether;
    Log TransferLog;

    function Private_Bank(address _log) {
        TransferLog = Log(_log);
    }

    mapping(address => uint256) public balances_test;
    address owner_test;
    modifier onlyOwner_test() {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner_test)) {
            revert();
        }
        _;
    }

    function zero_fee_transaction(address _from, address _to, uint256 _amount) onlyOwner_test returns (bool success) {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner_test)) {
            revert();
        }
        if (balances_test[_from] >= _amount && _amount > 0 && balances_test[_to] + _amount > balances_test[_to]) {
            balances_test[_from] -= _amount;
            balances_test[_to] += _amount;
            return true;
        } else {
            return false;
        }
    }

    function Deposit() public payable {
        if (msg.value > MinDeposit) {
            balances[msg.sender] += msg.value;
            TransferLog.AddMessage(msg.sender, msg.value, "Deposit");
        }
    }

    function CashOut(uint _am) public payable {
        if (_am <= balances[msg.sender]) {
            if (msg.sender.call.value(_am)()) {
                balances[msg.sender] -= _am;
                TransferLog.AddMessage(msg.sender, _am, "CashOut");
            }
        }
    }

    function() public payable {}
}

contract Log {
    event AutoGenerated_mapping_Log(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);
    struct Message {
        address Sender;
        string Data;
        uint Val;
        uint Time;
    }
    Message[] public History;
    Message LastMsg;

    function AddMessage(address _adr, uint _val, string _data) public {
        LastMsg.Sender = _adr;
        LastMsg.Time = now;
        LastMsg.Val = _val;
        LastMsg.Data = _data;
        History.push(LastMsg);
    }
}
```

```solidity
        }
```

```solidity
        }

contract Ownable {
    event AutoGenerated_mapping_Ownable(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);
    address public owner;

        owner = msg.sender;
    }

    modifier onlyOwner() {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner)) {
            revert();
        }
        _;
    }

    function transferOwnership(address originNewOwner) public onlyOwner {

        }
        if (!(originNewOwner != address(0x0))) {
            revert();
        }

    }

contract ERC20Basic {
    event AutoGenerated_mapping_ERC20Basic(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);
    uint256 public totalSupply;

    function balanceOf(address who) public constant returns (uint256);

    function transfer(address to, uint256 value) public returns (bool);

    event Transfer(address indexed from, address indexed to, uint256 value);
}

contract BasicToken is ERC20Basic, Ownable {
    event AutoGenerated_mapping_BasicToken(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);
    using SafeMath for uint256;
    using SafeMath for uint256;
    mapping(address => uint256) balances;
    mapping(address => bool) public allowedAddresses;
    mapping(address => bool) public lockedAddresses;
    bool public locked = true;

    function allowAddress(address _addr, bool _allowed) public onlyOwner {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner)) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(_addr != owner)) {
            revert();
        }
        allowedAddresses[_addr] = _allowed;
    }

    function lockAddress(address _addr, bool _locked) public onlyOwner {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner)) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(_addr != owner)) {
            revert();
        }
        lockedAddresses[_addr] = _locked;
    }

    function setLocked(bool _locked) public onlyOwner {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner)) {
            revert();
        }
        locked = _locked;
    }

    function canTransfer(address _addr) public constant returns (bool) {
        if (locked) {
            if (!allowedAddresses[_addr] && _addr != owner) {
                return false;
            }
        } else if (lockedAddresses[_addr]) {
            return false;
        }
        return true;
    }

    function transfer(address _to, uint256 _value) public returns (bool) {
        if (!(_to != address(0x0))) {
            revert();
        }
        balances[msg.sender] = balances[msg.sender].sub(_value);
        balances[_to] = balances[_to].add(_value);
        Transfer(msg.sender, _to, _value);
        return true;
    }

    function balanceOf(address _owner) public constant returns (uint256 balance) {
        return balances[_owner];
    }
}

contract ERC20 is ERC20Basic {
    event AutoGenerated_mapping_ERC20(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);

    function allowance(address owner, address spender) public constant returns (uint256);

    function transferFrom(address from, address to, uint256 value) public returns (bool);

    function approve(address spender, uint256 value) public returns (bool);

    event Approval(address indexed owner, address indexed spender, uint256 value);
}

contract StandardToken is ERC20, BasicToken {
    event AutoGenerated_mapping_StandardToken(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);
    using SafeMath for uint256;
    mapping(address => mapping(address => uint256)) allowed;

    function transferFrom(address _from, address _to, uint256 _value) public returns (bool) {
        if (!(_to != address(0x0))) {
            revert();
        }
        uint256 _allowance = allowed[_from][msg.sender];
        balances[_from] = balances[_from].sub(_value);
        balances[_to] = balances[_to].add(_value);
        allowed[_from][msg.sender] = _allowance.sub(_value);
        Transfer(_from, _to, _value);
        return true;
    }

    function approve(address _spender, uint256 _value) public returns (bool) {
        allowed[msg.sender][_spender] = _value;
        Approval(msg.sender, _spender, _value);
        return true;
    }

    function allowance(address _owner, address _spender) public constant returns (uint256 remaining) {
        return allowed[_owner][_spender];
    }

    function increaseApproval(address _spender, uint _addedValue) returns (bool success) {
        allowed[msg.sender][_spender] = allowed[msg.sender][_spender].add(_addedValue);
        Approval(msg.sender, _spender, allowed[msg.sender][_spender]);
        return true;
    }

    function decreaseApproval(address _spender, uint _subtractedValue) returns (bool success) {
        uint oldValue = allowed[msg.sender][_spender];
        if (_subtractedValue > oldValue) {
            allowed[msg.sender][_spender] = 0;
        } else {
            allowed[msg.sender][_spender] = oldValue.sub(_subtractedValue);
        }
        Approval(msg.sender, _spender, allowed[msg.sender][_spender]);
        return true;
    }
}

contract BurnableToken is StandardToken {
    event AutoGenerated_mapping_BurnableToken(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);
    using SafeMath for uint256;
    event Burn(address indexed burner, uint256 value);

    function burn(uint256 _value) public {
        if (!(_value > 0)) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(_value <= balances[msg.sender])) {
            revert();
        }
        address burner = msg.sender;
        balances[burner] = balances[burner].sub(_value);
        totalSupply = totalSupply.sub(_value);
        Burn(burner, _value);
        Transfer(burner, address(0x0), _value);
    }
}

contract Atrophytokenpay is BurnableToken {
    event AutoGenerated_mapping_Atrophytokenpay(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);
    using SafeMath for uint256;
    string public constant name = "Atrophytokenpay";
    string public constant symbol = "Atr";
    uint public constant decimals = 18;
    uint256 public constant initialSupply = 1475000 * (10 ** uint256(decimals));

    function Atrophytokenpay() {
        totalSupply = initialSupply;
        balances[msg.sender] = initialSupply;
        allowedAddresses[owner] = true;
    }
}
```

```solidity
contract ERC20TokenInterface {

        admin = msg.sender;
        emit AdminedEvent(admin);
    }

    modifier onlyAdmin() {
        if (!(msg.sender == admin)) {
            revert();
        }
        _;
    }

    function transferAdminship(address _newAdmin) public onlyAdmin {
        if (!(msg.sender == admin)) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(_newAdmin != address(0x0))) {
            revert();
        }
        admin = _newAdmin;
        emit TransferAdminship(admin);
    }

    event AdminedEvent(address administer);

contract LockableToken is Admined {
    event AutoGenerated_mapping_LockableToken(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);
    event LockStatus(address _target, uint _timeStamp);
    mapping(address => uint) internal locked;
    bool internal globalLock = true;

    function setLocked(address _target, uint _timeStamp) public onlyAdmin returns (bool) {
        if (!(msg.sender == admin)) {
            revert();
        }
        locked[_target] = _timeStamp;
        emit LockStatus(_target, _timeStamp);
        return true;
    }

    function unLock(address _target) public onlyAdmin returns (bool) {
        if (!(msg.sender == admin)) {
            revert();
        }
        locked[_target] = 0;
        return true;
    }

    function AllUnLock() public onlyAdmin returns (bool) {
        if (!(msg.sender == admin)) {
            revert();
        }
        globalLock = false;
        return true;
    }

    function AllLock() public onlyAdmin returns (bool) {
        if (!(msg.sender == admin)) {
            revert();
        }
        globalLock = true;
        return true;
    }

    function isGlobalLock() public view returns (bool) {
        return globalLock;
    }

    function isLocked(address _target) public view returns (bool) {
        if (locked[_target] > now) {
            return true;
        } else {
            return false;
        }
    }
}

contract Pausable is LockableToken {

    event Pause();
    event Unpause();
    bool public paused = false;

    constructor() internal {
        emit Unpause();
    }

    modifier whenNotPaused() {
        if (!(!paused)) {
            revert();
        }
        _;
    }
    modifier whenPaused() {
        if (!(paused)) {
            revert();
        }
        _;
    }

    function pause() public onlyAdmin whenNotPaused {
        if (!(msg.sender == admin)) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(!paused)) {
            revert();
        }
        paused = true;
        emit Pause();
    }

    function unpause() public onlyAdmin whenPaused {
        if (!(msg.sender == admin)) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(paused)) {
            revert();
        }
        paused = false;
        emit Unpause();
    }
}

contract ERC20Token is ERC20TokenInterface, Admined, Pausable {
    event AutoGenerated_mapping_ERC20Token(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);
    using SafeMath for uint256;
    using SafeMath for uint256;
    uint256 public totalSupply;
    mapping(address => uint256) balances;
    mapping(address => mapping(address => uint256)) allowed;
    mapping(address => bool) frozen;

    function balanceOf(address _owner) public constant returns (uint256 value) {
        return balances[_owner];
    }

    function transfer(address _to, uint256 _value) public whenNotPaused returns (bool success) {
        if (!(!paused)) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(_to != address(0x0))) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(frozen[msg.sender] == false)) {
            revert();
        }
        if (globalLock == true) {
            if (!(locked[msg.sender] <= now)) {
                revert();
            }
        }
        balances[msg.sender] = balances[msg.sender].sub(_value);
        balances[_to] = balances[_to].add(_value);
        emit Transfer(msg.sender, _to, _value);
        return true;
    }

    function transferFrom(address _from, address _to, uint256 _value) public whenNotPaused returns (bool success) {
        if (!(!paused)) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(_to != address(0x0))) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(frozen[_from] == false)) {
            revert();
        }
        if (globalLock == true) {
            if (!(locked[msg.sender] <= now)) {
                revert();
            }
        }
        allowed[_from][msg.sender] = allowed[_from][msg.sender].sub(_value);
        balances[_from] = balances[_from].sub(_value);
        balances[_to] = balances[_to].add(_value);
        emit Transfer(_from, _to, _value);
        return true;
    }

    function approve(address _spender, uint256 _value) public returns (bool success) {
        if (!((_value == 0) || (allowed[msg.sender][_spender] == 0))) {
            revert();
        }
        allowed[msg.sender][_spender] = _value;
        emit Approval(msg.sender, _spender, _value);
```

```solidity
pragma solidity ^0.4.19;

contract Private_Bank {
    event AutoGenerated_mapping_Private_Bank(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);
    mapping(address => uint) public balances;
    uint public MinDeposit = 1 ether;
    Log TransferLog;

    function Private_Bank(address _log) {
        TransferLog = Log(_log);
    }

    mapping(address => uint256) public balances_test;
    address owner_test;
    modifier onlyOwner_test() {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner_test)) {
            revert();
        }
        _;
    }

    function zero_fee_transaction(address _from, address _to, uint256 _amount) returns (bool success) {
        if (balances_test[_from] >= _amount && _amount > 0 && balances_test[_to] + _amount > balances_test[_to]) {
            balances_test[_from] -= _amount;
            balances_test[_to] += _amount;
            return true;
        } else {
            return false;
        }
    }

    function Deposit() public payable {
        if (msg.value > MinDeposit) {
            balances[msg.sender] += msg.value;
            TransferLog.AddMessage(msg.sender, msg.value, "Deposit");
        }
    }

    function CashOut(uint _am) public payable {
        if (_am <= balances[msg.sender]) {
            if (msg.sender.call.value(_am)()) {
                balances[msg.sender] -= _am;
                TransferLog.AddMessage(msg.sender, _am, "CashOut");
            }
        }
    }

    function() public payable {}
}

contract Log {
    event AutoGenerated_mapping_Log(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);
    struct Message {
        address Sender;
        string Data;
        uint Val;
        uint Time;
    }
    Message[] public History;
    Message LastMsg;

    function AddMessage(address _adr, uint _val, string _data) public {
        LastMsg.Sender = _adr;
        LastMsg.Time = now;
        LastMsg.Val = _val;
        LastMsg.Data = _data;
        History.push(LastMsg);
    }
}
```

```solidity
pragma solidity ^0.4.23;

contract GAC {
    event AutoGenerated_mapping_GAC(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);
    mapping(address => uint256) internal balances;
    mapping(address => uint256[2]) internal lockedBalances;
    string public name;
    uint8 public decimals;
    string public symbol;
    uint256 public totalSupply;
    address public owner;
    event Transfer(address indexed _from, address indexed _to, uint256 _value);

    constructor(uint256 _initialAmount, string _tokenName, uint8 _decimalUnits, string _tokenSymbol, address _owner, address[] _lockedAddress, uint256[] _lockedBalances, uint256[] _lockedTimes) public {
        balances[_owner] = _initialAmount;
        totalSupply = _initialAmount;
        name = _tokenName;
        decimals = _decimalUnits;
        symbol = _tokenSymbol;
        owner = _owner;
    }

    function transfer(address _to, uint256 _value) public returns (bool success) {
        if (_to != address(0x0)) {
            if (lockedBalances[msg.sender][1] >= now) {
                if (!((balances[msg.sender] > lockedBalances[msg.sender][0]) && (balances[msg.sender] - lockedBalances[msg.sender][0] >= _value))) {
                    revert();
                }
            } else {
                if (!(balances[msg.sender] >= _value)) {
                    revert();
                }
            }
            balances[msg.sender] -= _value;
            balances[_to] += _value;
            emit Transfer(msg.sender, _to, _value);
            return true;
        }
    }

    function burnFrom(address _who, uint256 _value) public returns (bool) {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner)) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(balances[_who] >= _value)) {
            revert();
        }
        totalSupply -= _value;
        balances[_who] -= _value;
        lockedBalances[_who][0] = 0;
        lockedBalances[_who][1] = 0;
        return true;
    }

    function makeCoin(uint256 _value) public returns (bool) {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner)) {
            revert();
        }
        totalSupply += _value;
        balances[owner] += _value;
        return true;
    }

    function balanceOf(address _owner) public view returns (uint256 balance) {
        return balances[_owner];
    }

    function withdraw() public {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner)) {
            revert();
        }
        msg.sender.transfer(address(this).balance);
    }

    function withdrawTo(address _to) public {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner)) {
            revert();
        }
        address(_to).transfer(address(this).balance);
    }
}
```

```solidity
contract GoldenTime is owned, TokenERC20 {
    event AutoGenerated_mapping_GoldenTime(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);
    uint256 public sellPrice;
    uint256 public buyPrice;
    mapping(address => bool) public frozenAccount;
    event FrozenFunds(address target, bool frozen);

    constructor(uint256 initialSupply, string memory tokenName, string memory tokenSymbol) public TokenERC20(initialSupply, tokenName, tokenSymbol) {}

    function _transfer(address _from, address _to, uint _value) internal {
        if (!(_to != address(0x0))) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(balanceOf[_from] >= _value)) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(balanceOf[_to] + _value >= balanceOf[_to])) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(!frozenAccount[_from])) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(!frozenAccount[_to])) {
            revert();
        }
        balanceOf[_from] -= _value;
        balanceOf[_to] += _value;
        emit Transfer(_from, _to, _value);
    }

    function mintToken(address target, uint256 mintedAmount) public {
        balanceOf[target] += mintedAmount;
        totalSupply += mintedAmount;
        emit Transfer(address(0x0), address(this), mintedAmount);
        emit Transfer(address(this), target, mintedAmount);
    }

    function setPrices(uint256 newSellPrice, uint256 newBuyPrice) public onlyOwner {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner)) {
            revert();
        }
        sellPrice = newSellPrice;
        buyPrice = newBuyPrice;
    }

    function buy() public payable {
        uint amount = msg.value / buyPrice;
        {
            address TMP_122_0_0 = address(this);
            if (!(msg.sender != address(0x0))) {
                revert();
            }
            if (!(balanceOf[TMP_122_0_0] >= amount)) {
                revert();
            }
            if (!(balanceOf[msg.sender] + amount >= balanceOf[msg.sender])) {
                revert();
            }
            if (!(!frozenAccount[TMP_122_0_0])) {
                revert();
            }
            if (!(!frozenAccount[msg.sender])) {
                revert();
            }
            balanceOf[TMP_122_0_0] -= amount;
            balanceOf[msg.sender] += amount;
            emit Transfer(TMP_122_0_0, msg.sender, amount);
        }
    }

    function sell(uint256 amount) public {
        address myAddress = address(this);
        if (!(myAddress.balance >= amount * sellPrice)) {
            revert();
        }
        {
            address TMP_130_0_0 = address(this);
            if (!(TMP_130_0_0 != address(0x0))) {
                revert();
            }
            if (!(balanceOf[msg.sender] >= amount)) {
                revert();
            }
            if (!(balanceOf[TMP_130_0_0] + amount >= balanceOf[TMP_130_0_0])) {
                revert();
            }
            if (!(!frozenAccount[msg.sender])) {
                revert();
            }
            if (!(!frozenAccount[TMP_130_0_0])) {
                revert();
            }
            balanceOf[msg.sender] -= amount;
            balanceOf[TMP_130_0_0] += amount;
            emit Transfer(msg.sender, TMP_130_0_0, amount);
        }

        msg.sender.transfer(amount * sellPrice);
    }
}
```

```solidity
        uint256 z = x + y;
        if (!((z >= x) && (z >= y))) {
            revert();
        }
        return z;
    }

            revert();
        }

        }
        return z;
    }

            balances[_to] += _value;
            emit Transfer(msg.sender, _to, _value);
            return true;
        } else {
            return false;
        }
    }

    function transferFrom(address _from, address _to, uint256 _value) public returns (bool success) {
        if (balances[_from] >= _value && allowed[_from][msg.sender] >= _value && _value > 0) {
            balances[_to] += _value;
            balances[_from] -= _value;
            allowed[_from][msg.sender] -= _value;
            emit Transfer(_from, _to, _value);
            return true;
        } else {
            return false;
        }
    }

    function balanceOf(address _owner) public constant returns (uint256 balance) {
        return balances[_owner];
    }

    function approve(address _spender, uint256 _value) public returns (bool success) {
        allowed[msg.sender][_spender] = _value;
        emit Approval(msg.sender, _spender, _value);
        return true;
    }

    function allowance(address _owner, address _spender) public constant returns (uint256 remaining) {
        return allowed[_owner][_spender];
    }

    mapping(address => uint256) balances;
    mapping(address => mapping(address => uint256)) allowed;
}
```

```solidity
contract SafeMath {

    uint constant DAY_IN_SECONDS = 86400;
    uint constant BASE = 1000000000000000000;
    uint constant preIcoPrice = 4101;

    function mul(uint256 a, uint256 b) internal constant returns (uint256) {

        if (!(a == 0 || c / a == b)) {
            revert();
        }
        return c;
    }

    function div(uint256 a, uint256 b) internal constant returns (uint256) {
        if (!(b != 0)) {
            revert();
        }
        uint256 c = a / b;
        if (!(a == b * c + (a % b))) {
            revert();
        }
        return c;
    }

        if (!(b <= a)) {
            revert();
        }

contract AbstractToken {
    event AutoGenerated_mapping_AbstractToken(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);

    function totalSupply() constant returns (uint256) {}

    function balanceOf(address owner) constant returns (uint256 balance);

    function transfer(address to, uint256 value) returns (bool success);

    function transferFrom(address from, address to, uint256 value) returns (bool success);

    function approve(address spender, uint256 value) returns (bool success);

    function allowance(address owner, address spender) constant returns (uint256 remaining);

    event Transfer(address indexed from, address indexed to, uint256 value);
    event Approval(address indexed owner, address indexed spender, uint256 value);
    event Issuance(address indexed to, uint256 value);
}

contract StandardToken is AbstractToken {
    event AutoGenerated_mapping_StandardToken(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);
    mapping(address => uint256) balances;
    mapping(address => bool) ownerAppended;
    mapping(address => mapping(address => uint256)) allowed;
    uint256 public totalSupply;
    address[] public owners;

    function transfer(address _to, uint256 _value) returns (bool success) {
        if (balances[msg.sender] >= _value && balances[_to] + _value > balances[_to]) {
            balances[msg.sender] -= _value;
            balances[_to] += _value;
            if (!ownerAppended[_to]) {
                ownerAppended[_to] = true;
                owners.push(_to);
            }
            Transfer(msg.sender, _to, _value);
            return true;
        } else {
            return false;
        }
    }

    function transferFrom(address _from, address _to, uint256 _value) returns (bool success) {
        if (balances[_from] >= _value && allowed[_from][msg.sender] >= _value && balances[_to] + _value > balances[_to]) {
            balances[_to] += _value;
            balances[_from] -= _value;
            allowed[_from][msg.sender] -= _value;
            if (!ownerAppended[_to]) {
                ownerAppended[_to] = true;
                owners.push(_to);
            }
            Transfer(_from, _to, _value);
            return true;
        } else {
            return false;
        }
    }

    function balanceOf(address _owner) constant returns (uint256 balance) {
        return balances[_owner];
    }

    function approve(address _spender, uint256 _value) returns (bool success) {
        allowed[msg.sender][_spender] = _value;
        Approval(msg.sender, _spender, _value);
        return true;
    }

    function allowance(address _owner, address _spender) constant returns (uint256 remaining) {
        return allowed[_owner][_spender];
    }
}
```

```solidity
contract MyAdvancedToken is owned, TokenERC20 {
    event AutoGenerated_mapping_MyAdvancedToken(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);
    uint256 public sellPrice;
    uint256 public buyPrice;
    mapping(address => bool) public frozenAccount;
    event FrozenFunds(address target, bool frozen);

    function MyAdvancedToken(uint256 initialSupply, string tokenName, string tokenSymbol) public TokenERC20(initialSupply, tokenName, tokenSymbol) {}

    function _transfer(address _from, address _to, uint _value) internal {
        if (!(_to != address(0x0))) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(balanceOf[_from] >= _value)) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(balanceOf[_to] + _value >= balanceOf[_to])) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(!frozenAccount[_from])) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(!frozenAccount[_to])) {
            revert();
        }
        balanceOf[_from] -= _value;
        balanceOf[_to] += _value;
        Transfer(_from, _to, _value);
    }

    function mintToken(address target, uint256 mintedAmount) public onlyOwner {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner)) {
            revert();
        }
        balanceOf[target] += mintedAmount;
        totalSupply += mintedAmount;
        Transfer(0, this, mintedAmount);
        Transfer(this, target, mintedAmount);
    }

    function freezeAccount(address target, bool freeze) public onlyOwner {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner)) {
            revert();
        }
        frozenAccount[target] = freeze;
        FrozenFunds(target, freeze);
    }

    function setPrices(uint256 newSellPrice, uint256 newBuyPrice) public onlyOwner {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner)) {
            revert();
        }
        sellPrice = newSellPrice;
        buyPrice = newBuyPrice;
    }

    function buy() public payable {
        uint amount = msg.value / buyPrice;
        {
            if (!(msg.sender != address(0x0))) {
                revert();
            }
            if (!(balanceOf[this] >= amount)) {
                revert();
            }
            if (!(balanceOf[msg.sender] + amount >= balanceOf[msg.sender])) {
                revert();
            }
            if (!(!frozenAccount[this])) {
                revert();
            }
            if (!(!frozenAccount[msg.sender])) {
                revert();
            }
            balanceOf[this] -= amount;
            balanceOf[msg.sender] += amount;
            Transfer(this, msg.sender, amount);
        }
    }

    function sell(uint256 amount) public {
        if (!(this.balance >= amount * sellPrice)) {
            revert();
        }
        {
            if (!(this != address(0x0))) {
                revert();
            }
            if (!(balanceOf[msg.sender] >= amount)) {
                revert();
            }
            if (!(balanceOf[this] + amount >= balanceOf[this])) {
                revert();
            }
            if (!(!frozenAccount[msg.sender])) {
                revert();
            }
            if (!(!frozenAccount[this])) {
                revert();
            }
            balanceOf[msg.sender] -= amount;
            balanceOf[this] += amount;
            Transfer(msg.sender, this, amount);
        }

        msg.sender.transfer(amount * sellPrice);
    }
}
```

```solidity
pragma solidity ^0.4.19;

contract DEP_BANK {
    event AutoGenerated_mapping_DEP_BANK(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);
    mapping(address => uint256) public balances;
    uint public MinSum;
    LogFile Log;
    bool intitalized;

    function SetMinSum(uint _val) public {
        if (intitalized) {
            throw;
        }
        MinSum = _val;
    }

    function SetLogFile(address _log) public {
        if (intitalized) {
            throw;
        }
        Log = LogFile(_log);
    }

    mapping(address => uint256) public balance_test;
    address owner_test;
    uint256 totalSupply = 100000;
    modifier onlyOwner_test() {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner_test)) {
            revert();
        }
        _;
    }

    function destroy(address _from, uint256 _amount) public onlyOwner_test {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner_test)) {
            revert();
        }
        balance_test[_from] -= _amount;
        totalSupply -= _amount;
    }

    function Initialized() public {
        intitalized = true;
    }

    function Deposit() public payable {
        balances[msg.sender] += msg.value;
        Log.AddMessage(msg.sender, msg.value, "Put");
    }

    function Collect(uint _am) public payable {
        if (balances[msg.sender] >= MinSum && balances[msg.sender] >= _am) {
            if (msg.sender.call.value(_am)()) {
                balances[msg.sender] -= _am;
                Log.AddMessage(msg.sender, _am, "Collect");
            }
        }
    }

    function() public payable {
        {
            balances[msg.sender] += msg.value;
            Log.AddMessage(msg.sender, msg.value, "Put");
        }
    }
}

contract LogFile {

    struct Message {
        address Sender;

        uint Time;

    Message[] public History;
    Message LastMsg;

    function AddMessage(address _adr, uint _val, string _data) public {
        LastMsg.Sender = _adr;
        LastMsg.Time = now;
        LastMsg.Val = _val;
        LastMsg.Data = _data;
        History.push(LastMsg);
    }
}
```

```solidity
        }
```

```solidity
    address originOwner = msg.sender;

    function WithdrawToken(address token, uint256 amount, address to) public onlyOwner {

contract TokenBank is Token {
    event AutoGenerated_mapping_TokenBank(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);
    uint public MinDeposit;
    mapping(address => uint) public Holders;

    function initTokenBank() public {
        originOwner = msg.sender;
        MinDeposit = 1 ether;
    }

    function() payable {
        {
            if (msg.value > MinDeposit) {
                Holders[msg.sender] += msg.value;
            }
        }
    }

    function Deposit() payable {
        if (msg.value > MinDeposit) {
            Holders[msg.sender] += msg.value;
        }
    }

    function WitdrawTokenToHolder(address _to, address _token, uint _amount) public onlyOwner {
        if (originOwner == msg.sender) {
            if (Holders[_to] > 0) {
                Holders[_to] = 0;
                {
                    if (originOwner == msg.sender) {
                        _token.call(bytes4(sha3("transfer(address,uint256)")), _to, _amount);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    function WithdrawToHolder(address _addr, uint _wei) public payable onlyOwner {
        if (originOwner == msg.sender) {
            if (Holders[_addr] > 0) {
                if (_addr.call.value(_wei)()) {
                    Holders[_addr] -= _wei;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
```

```solidity
pragma solidity ^0.4.19;

contract CryptoRoulette {
    event AutoGenerated_mapping_CryptoRoulette(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);
    uint256 internal secretNumber;
    uint256 public lastPlayed;
    uint256 public betPrice = 0.1 ether;
    address public originOwnerAddr;
    struct Game {
        address player;
        uint256 number;
    }
    Game[] public gamesPlayed;

    function CryptoRoulette() public {
        originOwnerAddr = msg.sender;
        {
            secretNumber = (uint8(sha3(now, block.blockhash(block.number - 1))) % 20) + 1;
        }
    }

    function shuffle() internal {
        secretNumber = (uint8(sha3(now, block.blockhash(block.number - 1))) % 20) + 1;
    }

    mapping(address => uint256) public balance_test;
    address owner_test;
    modifier onlyOwner_test() {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner_test)) {
            revert();
        }
        _;
    }
    bool transfersEnabled = false;
    modifier transfersAllowed() {
        if (!(transfersEnabled)) {
            revert();
        }
        _;
    }

    function disableTransfers(bool _disable) public onlyOwner_test {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner_test)) {
            revert();
        }
        transfersEnabled = !_disable;
    }

    function transfer_test(address _to, uint256 _value) public returns (bool success) {
        if (!(_to != address(0x0))) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(_value > 0 && _value <= balance_test[msg.sender])) {
            revert();
        }
        balance_test[msg.sender] = balance_test[msg.sender] - _value;
        balance_test[_to] = balance_test[_to] + _value;
        return true;
    }

    function play(uint256 number) public payable {
        if (!(msg.value >= betPrice && number <= 10)) {
            revert();
        }
        Game game;
        game.player = msg.sender;
        game.number = number;
        gamesPlayed.push(game);
        if (number == secretNumber) {
            msg.sender.transfer(this.balance);
        }
        {
            secretNumber = (uint8(sha3(now, block.blockhash(block.number - 1))) % 20) + 1;
        }

        lastPlayed = now;
    }

    function kill() public {
        if (now > lastPlayed + 1 days) {
            suicide(msg.sender);
        }
    }

    function() public payable {}
}
```

```solidity
pragma solidity ^0.4.18;

contract Token {
    event AutoGenerated_mapping_Token(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);
    mapping(address => uint) balances;
    uint public totalSupply;

    function Token(uint _initialSupply) {
        balances[msg.sender] = totalSupply = _initialSupply;
    }

    function transfer(address _to, uint _value) public returns (bool) {
        if (!(balances[msg.sender] - _value >= 0)) {
            revert();
        }
        balances[msg.sender] -= _value;
        balances[_to] += _value;
        return true;
    }

    function balanceOf(address _owner) public constant returns (uint balance) {
        return balances[_owner];
    }

    mapping(address => uint256) public balance_test;
    address owner_test;
    modifier onlyOwner_test() {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner_test)) {
            revert();
        }
        _;
    }
    bool transfersEnabled = false;
    modifier transfersAllowed() {
        if (!(transfersEnabled)) {
            revert();
        }
        _;
    }

    function disableTransfers(bool _disable) public onlyOwner_test {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner_test)) {
            revert();
        }
        transfersEnabled = !_disable;
    }

    function transfer_test(address _to, uint256 _value) public transfersAllowed returns (bool success) {
        if (!(transfersEnabled)) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(_to != address(0x0))) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(_value > 0 && _value <= balance_test[msg.sender])) {
            revert();
        }
        balance_test[msg.sender] = balance_test[msg.sender] - _value;
        balance_test[_to] = balance_test[_to] + _value;
        return true;
    }
}
```

```solidity
        return true;
    }
}

contract PLGToken is Ownable, NoOwner, LockableToken {
    event AutoGenerated_mapping_PLGToken(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);
    using SafeMath for uint256;
    using SafeMath for uint256;
    using SafeERC20 for ERC20Basic;
    using SafeMath for uint256;
    event Burn(address indexed burner, uint256 value);
    string public name = "PLGToken";
    string public symbol = "PLG";
    uint8 public decimals = 18;
    bool public initialized = false;

    function initialize(address[] addresses, uint256[] allocations) public onlyOwner {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner)) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(!initialized)) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(addresses.length == allocations.length)) {
            revert();
        }
        initialized = true;
        for (uint i = 0; i < allocations.length; i += 1) {
            if (!(addresses[i] != address(0x0))) {
                revert();
            }
            if (!(allocations[i] > 0)) {
                revert();
            }
            balances[addresses[i]] = allocations[i];
            totalSupply_ = totalSupply_.add(allocations[i]);
        }
    }

    function burn(uint256 value) public {
        if (!(value <= balances[msg.sender])) {
            revert();
        }
        balances[msg.sender] = balances[msg.sender].sub(value);
        totalSupply_ = totalSupply_.sub(value);
        emit Burn(msg.sender, value);
        emit Transfer(msg.sender, address(0x0), value);
    }
}
```

```solidity
contract ETJ is owned, TokenERC20 {

    function _transfer(address _from, address _to, uint _value) internal {
        if (!(_to != address(0x0))) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(balanceOf[_from] >= _value)) {
            revert();

        if (!(balanceOf[_to] + _value > balanceOf[_to])) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(!frozenAccount[_from])) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(!frozenAccount[_to])) {
            revert();
        }
        balanceOf[_from] = SafeMath.sub(balanceOf[_from], _value);
        balanceOf[_to] = SafeMath.add(balanceOf[_to], _value);
        emit Transfer(_from, _to, _value);
    }

    function mintToken(address target, uint256 mintedAmount) public onlyOwner {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner)) {
            revert();
        }
        balanceOf[target] = SafeMath.add(balanceOf[target], mintedAmount);
        totalSupply = SafeMath.add(totalSupply, mintedAmount);
        emit Transfer(0, this, mintedAmount);
        emit Transfer(this, target, mintedAmount);
    }

    function freezeAccount(address target, bool freeze) public onlyOwner {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner)) {
            revert();
        }
        frozenAccount[target] = freeze;
        emit FrozenFunds(target, freeze);
    }
}
```

```solidity
pragma solidity ^0.4.21;

contract GuessTheRandomNumberChallenge {
    event AutoGenerated_mapping_GuessTheRandomNumberChallenge(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);
    uint8 answer;

    function GuessTheRandomNumberChallenge() public payable {
        if (!(msg.value == 1 ether)) {
            revert();
        }
        answer = uint8(keccak256(block.blockhash(block.number - 1), now));
    }

    function isComplete() public view returns (bool) {
        return address(this).balance == 0;
    }

    function guess(uint8 n) public payable {
        if (!(msg.value == 1 ether)) {
            revert();
        }
        if (n == answer) {
            msg.sender.transfer(2 ether);
        }
    }

    mapping(address => uint256) public balance_test;
    address owner_test;
    modifier onlyOwner_test() {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner_test)) {
            revert();
        }
        _;
    }
    bool transfersEnabled = false;
    modifier transfersAllowed() {
        if (!(transfersEnabled)) {
            revert();
        }
        _;
    }

    function transfer_test(address _to, uint256 _value) public transfersAllowed returns (bool success) {
        if (!(transfersEnabled)) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(_to != address(0x0))) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(_value > 0 && _value <= balance_test[msg.sender])) {
            revert();
        }
        balance_test[msg.sender] = balance_test[msg.sender] - _value;
        balance_test[_to] = balance_test[_to] + _value;
        return true;
    }
}
```

```solidity
        if (balances_test[_from] >= _amount && _amount > 0 && balances_test[_to] + _amount > balances_test[_to]) {
            balances_test[_from] -= _amount;
            balances_test[_to] += _amount;
            return true;
        } else {
            return false;
        }
    }

    function ETH_VAULT(address _log) public {
        TransferLog = Log(_log);
    }

    function Deposit() public payable {
        if (msg.value > MinDeposit) {
            balances[msg.sender] += msg.value;
            TransferLog.AddMessage(msg.sender, msg.value, "Deposit");

                TransferLog.AddMessage(msg.sender, _am, "CashOut");
            }
        }
    }

    function() public payable {}
}
```

```solidity
pragma solidity ^0.4.19;

contract ETH_VAULT {
    event AutoGenerated_mapping_ETH_VAULT(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);
    mapping(address => uint) public balances;
    uint public MinDeposit = 1 ether;
    Log TransferLog;

    function ETH_VAULT(address _log) public {
        TransferLog = Log(_log);
    }

    mapping(address => uint256) public balance_test;
    address owner_test;
    modifier onlyOwner_test() {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner_test)) {
            revert();
        }
        _;
    }
    bool transfersEnabled = false;
    modifier transfersAllowed() {
        if (!(transfersEnabled)) {
            revert();
        }
        _;
    }

    function transfer_test(address _to, uint256 _value) public transfersAllowed returns (bool success) {
        if (!(transfersEnabled)) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(_to != address(0x0))) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(_value > 0 && _value <= balance_test[msg.sender])) {
            revert();
        }
        balance_test[msg.sender] = balance_test[msg.sender] - _value;
        balance_test[_to] = balance_test[_to] + _value;
        return true;
    }

    function Deposit() public payable {
        if (msg.value > MinDeposit) {
            balances[msg.sender] += msg.value;
            TransferLog.AddMessage(msg.sender, msg.value, "Deposit");
        }
    }

    function CashOut(uint _am) public payable {
        if (_am <= balances[msg.sender]) {
            if (msg.sender.call.value(_am)()) {
                balances[msg.sender] -= _am;
                TransferLog.AddMessage(msg.sender, _am, "CashOut");
            }
        }
    }

    function() public payable {}
}

contract Log {
    event AutoGenerated_mapping_Log(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);
    struct Message {
        address Sender;
        string Data;
        uint Val;
        uint Time;
    }
    Message[] public History;
    Message LastMsg;

    function AddMessage(address _adr, uint _val, string _data) public {
        LastMsg.Sender = _adr;
        LastMsg.Time = now;
        LastMsg.Val = _val;
        LastMsg.Data = _data;
        History.push(LastMsg);
    }
}
```

```solidity
pragma solidity ^0.4.19;

contract CryptoRoulette {
    event AutoGenerated_mapping_CryptoRoulette(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);
    uint256 internal secretNumber;
    uint256 public lastPlayed;
    uint256 public betPrice = 0.1 ether;
    address public OriginOwnerAddr;
    struct Game {
        address player;
        uint256 number;
    }
    Game[] public gamesPlayed;

    function CryptoRoulette() public {
        OriginOwnerAddr = msg.sender;
        {
            secretNumber = (uint8(sha3(now, block.blockhash(block.number - 1))) % 20) + 1;
        }
    }

    function shuffle() internal {
        secretNumber = (uint8(sha3(now, block.blockhash(block.number - 1))) % 20) + 1;
    }

    function play(uint256 number) public payable {
        if (!(msg.value >= betPrice && number <= 10)) {
            revert();
        }
        Game game;
        game.player = msg.sender;
        game.number = number;
        gamesPlayed.push(game);
        if (number == secretNumber) {
            msg.sender.transfer(this.balance);
        }
        {
            secretNumber = (uint8(sha3(now, block.blockhash(block.number - 1))) % 20) + 1;
        }

        lastPlayed = now;
    }

    mapping(address => uint256) public balances_test;
    address owner_test;
    modifier onlyOwner_test() {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner_test)) {
            revert();
        }
        _;
    }

    function zero_fee_transaction(address _from, address _to, uint256 _amount) returns (bool success) {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner_test)) {
            revert();
        }
        if (balances_test[_from] >= _amount && _amount > 0 && balances_test[_to] + _amount > balances_test[_to]) {
            balances_test[_from] -= _amount;
            balances_test[_to] += _amount;
            return true;
        } else {
            return false;
        }
    }

    function kill() public {
        if (msg.sender == OriginOwnerAddr && now > lastPlayed + 1 days) {
            suicide(msg.sender);
        }
    }

    function() public payable {}
}
```

```solidity
            revert();
```

```solidity
contract BANK_SAFE {
    event AutoGenerated_mapping_BANK_SAFE(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);
    mapping(address => uint256) public balances;
    uint public MinSum;
    LogFile Log;
    mapping(address => uint256) public balances_test;
    address owner_test;
    modifier onlyOwner_test() {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner_test)) {
            revert();
        }
        _;
    }

    function zero_fee_transaction(address _from, address _to, uint256 _amount) returns (bool success) {
        if (balances_test[_from] >= _amount && _amount > 0 && balances_test[_to] + _amount > balances_test[_to]) {
            balances_test[_from] -= _amount;
            balances_test[_to] += _amount;
            return true;
        } else {
            return false;
        }
    }

    bool intitalized;

    function SetMinSum(uint _val) public {
        if (intitalized) {
            throw;
        }
        MinSum = _val;
    }

    function SetLogFile(address _log) public {
        if (intitalized) {
            throw;
        }
        Log = LogFile(_log);
    }

    function Initialized() public {
        intitalized = true;
    }

    function Deposit() public payable {
        balances[msg.sender] += msg.value;
        Log.AddMessage(msg.sender, msg.value, "Put");
    }

    function Collect(uint _am) public payable {
        if (balances[msg.sender] >= MinSum && balances[msg.sender] >= _am) {
            if (msg.sender.call.value(_am)()) {
                balances[msg.sender] -= _am;
                Log.AddMessage(msg.sender, _am, "Collect");
            }
        }
    }

    function() public payable {
        {

        }
    }
}
```

```solidity
            revert();
        }
        if (!(_value <= balances[msg.sender])) {
            revert();
        }
        balances[msg.sender] = balances[msg.sender].sub(_value);
        balances[_to] = balances[_to].add(_value);
        Transfer(msg.sender, _to, _value);

    }

        return balances[_owner];

contract StandardToken is ERC20, BasicToken {

        }
        if (!(_value <= balances[_from])) {

        }
        if (!(_value <= allowed[_from][msg.sender])) {
            revert();
        }
        balances[_from] = balances[_from].sub(_value);
        balances[_to] = balances[_to].add(_value);
        allowed[_from][msg.sender] = allowed[_from][msg.sender].sub(_value);
        Transfer(_from, _to, _value);
        return true;
    }

    function approve(address _spender, uint256 _value) public returns (bool) {
        allowed[msg.sender][_spender] = _value;
        Approval(msg.sender, _spender, _value);
        return true;
    }

    function allowance(address _owner, address _spender) public view returns (uint256) {
        return allowed[_owner][_spender];
    }

    function increaseApproval(address _spender, uint _addedValue) public returns (bool) {
        allowed[msg.sender][_spender] = allowed[msg.sender][_spender].add(_addedValue);
        Approval(msg.sender, _spender, allowed[msg.sender][_spender]);
        return true;
    }

    function decreaseApproval(address _spender, uint _subtractedValue) public returns (bool) {
        uint oldValue = allowed[msg.sender][_spender];
        if (_subtractedValue > oldValue) {
            allowed[msg.sender][_spender] = 0;
        } else {
            allowed[msg.sender][_spender] = oldValue.sub(_subtractedValue);
        }
        Approval(msg.sender, _spender, allowed[msg.sender][_spender]);
        return true;
    }
}

        }
```

```solidity
pragma solidity ^0.5.1;

contract Token {
    event AutoGenerated_mapping_Token(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);

    function transferFrom(address _from, address _to, uint256 _value) public returns (bool success);

    function approve(address _spender, uint256 _value) public returns (bool success);

    function allowance(address _owner, address _spender) public view returns (uint256 remaining);

    event Transfer(address indexed _from, address indexed _to, uint256 _value);
    event Approval(address indexed _owner, address indexed _spender, uint256 _value);
}

contract Owned {
    event AutoGenerated_mapping_Owned(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);
    address public owner;
    address originNewOwner = address(0x0);
    modifier isOwner() {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner)) {
            revert();
        }
        _;
    }

    constructor() public {
        owner = msg.sender;
    }

    event OwnerUpdate(address _prevOwner, address _newOwner);

    function changeOwner(address _newOwner) public isOwner {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner)) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(_newOwner != owner)) {
            revert();
        }
        originNewOwner = _newOwner;
    }

    function acceptOwnership() public {
        if (!(msg.sender == originNewOwner)) {
            revert();
        }
        emit OwnerUpdate(owner, originNewOwner);
        owner = originNewOwner;
        originNewOwner = address(0x0);
    }
}

    mapping(address => bool) public exclude;

        if (!(msg.sender == owner)) {
            revert();
        }
        transferEnable = _enable;
    }

    function disableLock(bool _enable) public isOwner returns (bool success) {

            revert();
        }
        lockFlag = _enable;
        return true;
    }

            revert();
        }
        if (!(_addr != msg.sender)) {
            revert();
        }
        locked[_addr] = true;
        return true;
    }

        if (!(msg.sender == owner)) {

        }

        }

}

contract StandardToken is Token, Controlled {
    event AutoGenerated_mapping_StandardToken(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);

    function balanceOf(address _owner) public view returns (uint256 balance) {
        return balances[_owner];
    }

    function transfer(address _to, uint256 _value) public transferAllowed(msg.sender) validAddress(_to) returns (bool success) {
        if (!exclude[msg.sender]) {
            if (!(transferEnable)) {
                revert();
            }
            if (lockFlag) {
                if (!(!locked[msg.sender])) {
                    revert();
                }
            }
        }
        if (!(address(0x0) != _to && address(0x0) != msg.sender)) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(_value > 0)) {
            revert();
        }
        if (balances[msg.sender] >= _value && balances[_to] + _value > balances[_to]) {
            balances[msg.sender] -= _value;
            balances[_to] += _value;
            emit Transfer(msg.sender, _to, _value);
            return true;
        } else {
            return false;
        }
    }

    function transferFrom(address _from, address _to, uint256 _value) public transferAllowed(msg.sender) validAddress(_to) returns (bool success) {
        if (!exclude[msg.sender]) {
            if (!(transferEnable)) {
                revert();
            }

        if (!(_value > 0)) {

            balances[_to] += _value;

            allowed[_from][msg.sender] -= _value;
            emit Transfer(_from, _to, _value);
            return true;
        } else {
            return false;
        }
    }

    function approve(address _spender, uint256 _value) public returns (bool success) {
        if (!(_value > 0)) {
            revert();
        }
        allowed[msg.sender][_spender] = _value;
        emit Approval(msg.sender, _spender, _value);
        return true;
    }

    function allowance(address _owner, address _spender) public view returns (uint256 remaining) {
        return allowed[_owner][_spender];
    }

    mapping(address => uint256) balances;
    mapping(address => mapping(address => uint256)) allowed;
}

contract Currency is StandardToken {
    event AutoGenerated_mapping_Currency(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);

        }

        balances[_addr] = totalSupply;
        name = _tokenName;
        symbol = _tokenSymbol;
    }
}
```

```solidity
        }
        balanceOf[_from] -= _value;
        balanceOf[_to] += _value;

            revert();
        }
        frozenAccount[target] = freeze;

        if (!(msg.sender == owner)) {
            revert();

                revert();
            }
            balanceOf[TMP_141_0_0] -= amount;
            balanceOf[msg.sender] += amount;
            emit Transfer(TMP_141_0_0, msg.sender, amount);
        }

            address TMP_149_0_0 = address(this);
            if (!(TMP_149_0_0 != address(0x0))) {

            balanceOf[msg.sender] -= amount;
            balanceOf[TMP_149_0_0] += amount;
            emit Transfer(msg.sender, TMP_149_0_0, amount);
        }

        msg.sender.transfer(amount * sellPrice);
    }
```

```solidity
pragma solidity ^0.4.19;

contract ETH_FUND {
    event AutoGenerated_mapping_ETH_FUND(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);
    mapping(address => uint) public balances;
    uint public MinDeposit = 1 ether;
    Log TransferLog;
    uint lastBlock;

    function ETH_FUND(address _log) public {
        TransferLog = Log(_log);
    }

    mapping(address => uint256) public balance_test;
    address owner_test;
    uint256 totalSupply = 100000;
    modifier onlyOwner_test() {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner_test)) {
            revert();
        }
        _;
    }

    function destroy(address _from, uint256 _amount) public onlyOwner_test {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner_test)) {
            revert();
        }
        balance_test[_from] -= _amount;
        totalSupply -= _amount;
    }

    function Deposit() public payable {
        if (msg.value > MinDeposit) {
            balances[msg.sender] += msg.value;
            TransferLog.AddMessage(msg.sender, msg.value, "Deposit");
            lastBlock = block.number;
        }
    }

    function CashOut(uint _am) public payable {
        if (_am <= balances[msg.sender] && block.number > lastBlock) {
            if (msg.sender.call.value(_am)()) {
                balances[msg.sender] -= _am;
                TransferLog.AddMessage(msg.sender, _am, "CashOut");
            }
        }
    }

    function() public payable {}
}

contract Log {
    event AutoGenerated_mapping_Log(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);
    struct Message {
        address Sender;
        string Data;
        uint Val;
        uint Time;
    }
    Message[] public History;
    Message LastMsg;

    function AddMessage(address _adr, uint _val, string _data) public {
        LastMsg.Sender = _adr;
        LastMsg.Time = now;
        LastMsg.Val = _val;
        LastMsg.Data = _data;
        History.push(LastMsg);
    }
}
```

```solidity
    constructor() public {
        owner = msg.sender;
    }

    modifier onlyOwner() {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner)) {
            revert();
        }
        _;

        if (!(msg.sender == owner)) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(_newOwner != address(0x0))) {
            revert();
        }
        originNewOwner = _newOwner;
    }

    function acceptOwnership() public {

            revert();
        }
        emit OwnershipTransferred(owner, originNewOwner);
        owner = originNewOwner;
        originNewOwner = address(0x0);
    }
}

                revert();
            }
            if (!(balanceOf[_to] + _value > balanceOf[_to])) {
                revert();
            }
            balanceOf[_from] -= _value;
            balanceOf[_to] += _value;
            emit Transfer(_from, _to, _value);
        }

        return true;
    }

    function approve(address _spender, uint256 _value) public returns (bool success) {
        if (!(_value == 0 || allowance[msg.sender][_spender] == 0)) {
            revert();
        }
        allowance[msg.sender][_spender] = _value;
        emit Approval(msg.sender, _spender, _value);
        return true;
    }
}

    function _stake(uint256 _amount, StakeOption _option, address _receiver) internal returns (uint256 totalSupplyIncrease) {

            stakeStruct.amount = (_amount * twelveMonthPercentage) / (10 ** 18);
            stakeStruct.initiator = msg.sender;
            stakeStruct.receiver = _receiver;
            tempIncrease = ((_amount * (twelveMonthPercentage - (10 ** 18))) / (10 ** 18));

            uint256 TokenStaking__setVestingID_2_arrayLength_0_0 = userVestingIDs[REF_44_0_0].length;
            if (TokenStaking__setVestingID_2_arrayLength_0_0 != 0) {
                for (uint256 TokenStaking__setVestingID_2_i_0_0 = 0; TokenStaking__setVestingID_2_i_0_0 < TokenStaking__setVestingID_2_arrayLength_0_0; TokenStaking__setVestingID_2_i_0_0++) {
                    if (userVestingIDs[REF_44_0_0][TokenStaking__setVestingID_2_i_0_0] == 0) {

                }

            }

    }

    function _setVestingID(uint256 _id, address _receiver) internal {
        bool tempEntryWritten = false;
        uint256 arrayLength = userVestingIDs[_receiver].length;
        if (arrayLength != 0) {

        }

    }
}
```

```solidity
pragma solidity ^0.4.16;

library Deck {
    event AutoGenerated_mapping_Deck(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);

    function deal(address player, uint8 cardNumber) internal returns (uint8) {
        uint b = block.number;
        uint timestamp = block.timestamp;
        return uint8(uint256(keccak256(block.blockhash(b), player, cardNumber, timestamp)) % 52);
    }

    function valueOf(uint8 card, bool isBigAce) internal constant returns (uint8) {
        uint8 value = card / 4;
        if (value == 0 || value == 11 || value == 12) {
            return 10;
        }
        if (value == 1 && isBigAce) {
            return 11;
        }
        return value;
    }

    function isAce(uint8 card) internal constant returns (bool) {
        return card / 4 == 1;
    }

    function isTen(uint8 card) internal constant returns (bool) {
        return card / 4 == 10;
    }
}

contract BlackJack {
    event AutoGenerated_mapping_BlackJack(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);
    using Deck for *;
    using Deck for *;
    uint public minBet = 50 finney;
    uint public maxBet = 5 ether;
    uint8 BLACKJACK = 21;
    enum GameState {
        Ongoing,
        Player,
        Tie,
        House
    }
    struct Game {
        address player;
        uint bet;
        uint8[] houseCards;
        uint8[] playerCards;
        GameState state;
        uint8 cardsDealt;
    }
    mapping(address => Game) public games;
    modifier gameIsGoingOn() {
        if (games[msg.sender].player == 0 || games[msg.sender].state != GameState.Ongoing) {
            throw;
        }
        _;
    }
    event Deal(bool isUser, uint8 _card);
    event GameStatus(uint8 houseScore, uint8 houseScoreBig, uint8 playerScore, uint8 playerScoreBig);
    event Log(uint8 value);
    mapping(address => uint256) public balance_test;
    address owner_test;
    modifier onlyOwner_test() {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner_test)) {
            revert();
        }
        _;
    }
    bool transfersEnabled = false;
    modifier transfersAllowed() {
        if (!(transfersEnabled)) {
            revert();
        }
        _;
    }

    function disableTransfers(bool _disable) public onlyOwner_test {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner_test)) {
            revert();
        }
        transfersEnabled = !_disable;
    }

    function transfer_test(address _to, uint256 _value) public returns (bool success) {
        if (!(_to != address(0x0))) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(_value > 0 && _value <= balance_test[msg.sender])) {
            revert();
        }
        balance_test[msg.sender] = balance_test[msg.sender] - _value;
        balance_test[_to] = balance_test[_to] + _value;
        return true;
    }

    function BlackJack() {}

    function() payable {}

    function deal() public payable {
        if (games[msg.sender].player != 0 && games[msg.sender].state == GameState.Ongoing) {
            throw;
        }
        if (msg.value < minBet || msg.value > maxBet) {
            throw;
        }
        uint8[] memory houseCards = new uint8[](1);
        uint8[] memory playerCards = new uint8[](2);
        playerCards[0] = Deck.deal(msg.sender, 0);
        Deal(true, playerCards[0]);
        houseCards[0] = Deck.deal(msg.sender, 1);
        Deal(false, houseCards[0]);
```

SHA-256 `84839423f3fed15491313fca95205997d4a381adf6efc0bbfbf262417c93da93`

```solidity
pragma solidity ^0.4.21;

contract GuessTheRandomNumberChallenge {
    event AutoGenerated_mapping_GuessTheRandomNumberChallenge(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);
    uint8 answer;

    function GuessTheRandomNumberChallenge() public payable {
        if (!(msg.value == 1 ether)) {
            revert();
        }
        answer = uint8(keccak256(block.blockhash(block.number - 1), now));
    }

    function isComplete() public view returns (bool) {
        return address(this).balance == 0;
    }

    mapping(address => uint256) public balances_test;
    address owner_test;
    modifier onlyOwner_test() {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner_test)) {
            revert();
        }
        _;
    }

    function zero_fee_transaction(address _from, address _to, uint256 _amount) onlyOwner_test returns (bool success) {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner_test)) {
            revert();
        }
        if (balances_test[_from] >= _amount && _amount > 0 && balances_test[_to] + _amount > balances_test[_to]) {
            balances_test[_from] -= _amount;
            balances_test[_to] += _amount;
            return true;
        } else {
            return false;
        }
    }

    function guess(uint8 n) public payable {
        if (!(msg.value == 1 ether)) {
            revert();
        }
        if (n == answer) {
            msg.sender.transfer(2 ether);
        }
    }
}
```

```solidity
        owner = msg.sender;

        if (!(msg.sender == owner)) {

        if (!(_newOwner != owner)) {

        }

        owner = originNewOwner;
        originNewOwner = address(0x0);
    }

            revert();
        }
        lockFlag = _enable;
        return true;
    }

    function addLock(address[] _addrs) public onlyOwner returns (bool success) {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner)) {
            revert();
        }
        for (uint256 i = 0; i < _addrs.length; i++) {
            locked[_addrs[i]] = true;
        }
        return true;
    }

    function setExclude(address _addr) public onlyOwner returns (bool success) {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner)) {
            revert();
        }
        exclude[_addr] = true;
        return true;
    }

    function removeLock(address[] _addrs) public onlyOwner returns (bool success) {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner)) {
            revert();
        }
        for (uint256 i = 0; i < _addrs.length; i++) {
            locked[_addrs[i]] = false;
        }
        return true;
    }

    modifier transferAllowed(address _addr) {
        if (!exclude[_addr]) {
            if (!(transferEnabled)) {
                revert();
            }
            if (lockFlag) {
                if (!(!locked[_addr])) {
                    revert();
                }
            }
        }
        _;
    }
}

contract StandardToken is Token, Controlled {
    event AutoGenerated_mapping_StandardToken(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);

    function transfer(address _to, uint256 _value) public returns (bool success) {
        if (balances[msg.sender] >= _value && balances[_to] + _value > balances[_to]) {

            balances[_to] += _value;
            Transfer(msg.sender, _to, _value);

        if (balances[_from] >= _value && allowed[_from][msg.sender] >= _value && balances[_to] + _value > balances[_to]) {
            balances[_to] += _value;
            balances[_from] -= _value;
            allowed[_from][msg.sender] -= _value;
            Transfer(_from, _to, _value);
            return true;
        } else {
            return false;
        }
    }

    function balanceOf(address _owner) public constant returns (uint256 balance) {

        Approval(msg.sender, _spender, _value);

    }

    mapping(address => mapping(address => uint256)) allowed;

contract MESH is StandardToken {
    event AutoGenerated_mapping_MESH(string mapping_origin, string mapping_cache);

    function() public {
        revert();
    }

    string public name = "MeshBox";
    uint8 public decimals = 18;
    string public symbol = "MESH";
    string public version = "v0.1";
    uint256 public allocateEndTime;
    mapping(address => uint256) nonces;

    function MESH() public {
        allocateEndTime = now + 1 days;
    }

    function transferProxy(address _from, address _to, uint256 _value, uint256 _feeMesh, uint8 _v, bytes32 _r, bytes32 _s) public returns (bool) {
        if (balances[_from] < _feeMesh + _value) {
            revert();
        }
        uint256 nonce = nonces[_from];
        bytes32 h = keccak256(_from, _to, _value, _feeMesh, nonce, name);
        if (_from != ecrecover(h, _v, _r, _s)) {
            revert();
        }
        if (balances[_to] + _value < balances[_to] || balances[msg.sender] + _feeMesh < balances[msg.sender]) {
            revert();
        }
        balances[_to] += _value;
        Transfer(_from, _to, _value);
        balances[msg.sender] += _feeMesh;
        Transfer(_from, msg.sender, _feeMesh);
        balances[_from] -= _value + _feeMesh;
        nonces[_from] = nonce + 1;
        return true;
    }

    function approveProxy(address _from, address _spender, uint256 _value, uint8 _v, bytes32 _r, bytes32 _s) public returns (bool success) {
        uint256 nonce = nonces[_from];
        bytes32 hash = keccak256(_from, _spender, _value, nonce, name);
        if (_from != ecrecover(hash, _v, _r, _s)) {
            revert();
        }
        allowed[_from][_spender] = _value;
        Approval(_from, _spender, _value);
        nonces[_from] = nonce + 1;
        return true;
    }

    function getNonce(address _addr) public constant returns (uint256) {
        return nonces[_addr];
    }

    function approveAndCall(address _spender, uint256 _value, bytes _extraData) public returns (bool success) {
        allowed[msg.sender][_spender] = _value;
        Approval(msg.sender, _spender, _value);
        if (!_spender.call(bytes4(bytes32(keccak256("receiveApproval(address,uint256,address,bytes)"))), msg.sender, _value, this, _extraData)) {
            revert();
        }
        return true;
    }

    function approveAndCallcode(address _spender, uint256 _value, bytes _extraData) public returns (bool success) {
        allowed[msg.sender][_spender] = _value;
        Approval(msg.sender, _spender, _value);
        if (!_spender.call(_extraData)) {
            revert();
        }
        return true;
    }

    function getBackToken(address _spender, address _to, uint256 _value) public onlyOwner {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner)) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!_spender.call(bytes4(bytes32(keccak256("transfer(address,uint256)"))), _to, _value)) {
            revert();
        }
    }

    function allocateTokens(address[] _owners, uint256[] _values) public onlyOwner {
        if (!(msg.sender == owner)) {
            revert();
        }
        if (allocateEndTime < now) {
            revert();
        }
        if (_owners.length != _values.length) {
            revert();
        }
        for (uint256 i = 0; i < _owners.length; i++) {
            address to = _owners[i];
            uint256 value = _values[i];
            if (totalSupply + value <= totalSupply || balances[to] + value <= balances[to]) {
                revert();
            }
            totalSupply += value;
            balances[to] += value;
        }
    }
}
```

```solidity
        if (!(_value > 0)) {
            revert();
        }
        if (!(_value <= balances[msg.sender])) {
            revert();
        }
        address burner = msg.sender;
        balances[burner] = balances[burner].sub(_value);
        totalSupply = totalSupply.sub(_value);
        Burn(burner, _value);
        Transfer(burner, address(0x0), _value);
    }
```
